Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

中文摘要翻译: 在部分可观测强化学习中，智能体面临双重瓶颈：它们必须进行探索以遇到可获得奖励的状态，并在记忆中保留这些经验以优化其策略。探索奖励和记忆架构传统上被孤立地评估，导致二者之间的交互未被测量，而标准的稀疏奖励概念将时间信号密度与奖励实际所监督的内容混为一谈。我们提出了一项受控研究，在三个环境（其记忆内容的获取方式各不相同）中交叉使用情节探索奖励与多种神经记忆架构。相同的奖励信号产生了三种不同的交互模式：在记忆内容必须被主动发现并无监督保留的环境中，奖励放大了架构容量差异；在记忆内容一旦被寻找即为单一奖励监督线索的环境中，奖励将各架构拉平到共同上限；而在观测流纯属计划性安排的环境中，奖励完全无效。受控奖励操控验证了这些模式追踪的是奖励结构而非密度：密集奖励仅当其直接监督所需的潜在记忆时才中和探索奖励，而探索动作上小额可避免的惩罚（不改变最优解）会诱导策略收敛到次优静态状态，任一探索奖励均可解决此问题。我们随后用观测锚定的奖励机形式化稀疏性，将结构稀疏性（自动机无需任务所需历史即可复现回报）与潜在稀疏性（单步奖励对局部探索动作错误定价）区分开来；所得词汇体系根据每个任务暴露的记忆保留负担对三种模式进行了分类。综合来看，这些结果表明探索与记忆是互补而非替代关系：奖励仅诱导暴露，而只有记忆能将暴露转化为回报。

</details>


### [219] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的符号回归（SR）框架，将大语言模型嵌入迭代符号回归算法中，用于自动化动力学模型发现。LLM在每轮迭代中扮演两个角色：对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，以及基于SR生成模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个异质催化和生物过程案例中，该方法相比最先进的SR框架减少了41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数，且预测性能相当（R²>0.98）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 符号回归在动力学模型发现中通常不依赖领域知识，往往探索出物理化学上不合理的模型。LLM有望将领域专业知识注入搜索过程，减少模型发现的迭代次数和实验成本。

Method: 提出一个LLM引导的符号回归框架，将LLM模块嵌入迭代式SR算法中。LLM在每个迭代中承担两个功能：(1) 对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判；(2) 基于SR生成模型和嵌入的化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。

Result: 在四个复杂度递增的in silico案例（涵盖异质催化和生物过程系统）上，LLM引导框架相比最先进的SR框架减少了41.7%-79.3%的迭代次数；LLM在超过一半的引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构；独立验证集上两种方法预测性能相当，所有案例R²>0.98；消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模均贡献于性能，缩减规模的LLM在很大程度上保留了发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM能有效地将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，显著减少寻找地面真值模型所需的迭代次数和实验工作量，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 动力学模型发现是化学工程中的一个核心挑战，因为准确的速率表达式对于理解和控制化学与生物过程至关重要。符号回归（SR）已成为识别可解释动力学模型的有力数据驱动方法，但通常不利用领域知识，往往探索物理化学上不合理的模型。大语言模型（LLM）为将领域专业知识注入这一搜索过程提供了有前景的途径。在此，我们介绍了一个LLM引导的符号回归框架，将LLM模块嵌入迭代式SR算法中，用于自动化动力学模型发现。LLM在每次迭代中扮演两个角色：（1）对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，（2）基于SR生成的模型和嵌入的化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。我们在四个复杂度递增的in silico案例研究中评估了该框架，涵盖异质催化和生物过程系统。结果表明，与最先进的SR框架相比，LLM引导框架识别地面真值模型所需的迭代次数减少了41.7%-79.3%，且LLM在超过一半的引导运行中直接提出了正确的模型结构。在实际场景中，每次迭代通常需要新的湿实验，这意味着实验工作量的显著减少。在独立验证集上的预测性能在两种方法之间相当，所有案例研究中R²>0.98。消融研究表明，SR组件和LLM规模均对性能有所贡献，缩减规模的LLM在很大程度上保留了发现效率。这些发现表明，LLM可以有效地将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平了道路。

</details>


### [220] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSTQ框架，通过过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知一维量化，在分布式优化中实现局部差分隐私的同时保持低位通信成本，并达到最优均方误差缩放。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在分布式优化中同时实现局部差分隐私和低通信成本仍然具有挑战性。现有的向量量化方法（如vqSGD）依赖高维几何构造，但会引入不利的维度相关方差。

Method: 提出Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ)框架，结合了过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知的一维量化。包含两个变体：Flat Randomized Response版本和Metric-Aware Laplace版本（后者更适合高码本位宽场景）。同时推导了一个隐私感知的码本目标函数，将码本相关的MSE缩放从O(4^b)降至O(2^b)。

Result: SSTQ仅使用⌈log₂N⌉+b比特每客户端（N=Θ(d)为框架大小）即可达到最优均方误差缩放；码本目标函数将MSE缩放从O(4^b)降至O(2^b)；在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的联邦学习任务中优于现有基线，展现出良好的效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: SSTQ通过结合过完备框架、子采样和低维量化，有效解决了差分隐私分布式优化中通信成本与隐私-效用权衡的难题，理论分析和实验均验证了其优越性。

Original Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 在分布式优化中实现局部差分隐私的同时保持低通信成本仍然具有挑战性。现有的向量量化方法，如vqSGD，使用高维几何构造但会带来不利的维度相关方差。在本工作中，我们提出了子采样随机TurboQuant（SSTQ）框架，该框架结合了过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样以及隐私感知的一维量化。SSTQ包含两个变体：扁平随机响应版本和度量感知拉普拉斯版本，后者更适合较高的码本位宽场景。我们证明SSTQ仅使用每客户端⌈log₂N⌉+b比特（其中N=Θ(d)为框架大小）即可达到最优均方误差缩放。我们还推导了一个隐私感知的码本目标函数，将码本相关的MSE缩放从O(4^b)降至O(2^b)。最后，我们在使用CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST的联邦学习任务中将SSTQ与既有基线进行了实证评估，展示了其优越的效用和通信效率。

</details>


### [221] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，梯度下降在因子化模型W=UV^⊤上隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam不具备此性质。原因在于Adam不是规范等变的（gauge-equivariant），无法利用梯度流通过规范对称性实现的低秩恢复机制。作者证明了无记忆等变规则恰好等价于Gram确定左预条件子，并系统比较了九种更新规则，发现从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族可以单调恢复低秩偏置，证明各向异性是根本原因。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究已观察到梯度下降在因子化模型中对低秩解有隐式偏置，而Adam从相同初始化出发却没有这一偏置。理解这一差异的根源对于选择优化器以及在何种条件下可以恢复低秩最优解至关重要，但目前缺乏系统的理论解释和实验验证。

Method: （1）通过规范对称性（gauge symmetry）分析损失在(U,V)→(UQ,VQ)变换下的不变性，论证优化器必须满足规范等变才能继承梯度流的低秩机制；（2）提出结构定理，刻画无记忆等变规则恰好为Gram确定左预条件子；（3）提出传递定理，将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流；（4）在欠定矩阵感知任务上对九种更新规则进行系统比较，并引入"谱调度"（spectral schedule）调和关于Muon的相互矛盾的研究结果；（5）在Transformer和超光谱数据集上进行验证实验。

Result: （1）梯度下降、动量、"共享标量"Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足规范等变性，而Adam、RMSProp及其他坐标级方法不满足；（2）构造从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族，单调恢复低秩偏置，证明各向异性是原因；（3）Muon在等速率更新时可精确恢复低秩目标，但在谱尾增长时优势消失；（4）Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价的初始化，最终每个头的不变量W_Q^⊤W_K相对Frobenius距离相差56%；（5）在两个超光谱数据集上，在匹配训练损失条件下，梯度下降在最低采样密度和更低有效秩处将留出错误降低43-44%。

Conclusion: 基座选择（basis choice）不是优化器的调参细节，而是一个决定优化器选择哪个插值函数的根本性决策。只有规范等变的优化器才能继承梯度流的低秩恢复路径性质，这为实际应用中优化器的选择提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

中文摘要翻译: 梯度下降在因子化模型W = UV^⊤上隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备这一性质。我们将差异追溯到损失的规范对称性，即其在(U, V) ↦ (UQ, VQ)变换下的不变性。梯度流的低秩机制仅当优化器满足规范等变性时才对其可用，该条件是机制传递的必要条件但非充分条件。梯度下降、动量、"共享标量"Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足此条件；Adam、RMSProp及其他坐标级方法不满足。一个结构定理刻画了无记忆等变规则恰好等价于Gram确定的左预条件子，一个传递定理将梯度流的路径性质推广至公共标量流。随后我们在欠定矩阵感知中依据恢复误差对九种更新规则进行排序。一个从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族单调地恢复该偏置，孤立出各向异性为其成因。一个"谱调度"调和了关于Muon的两项相互矛盾的研究结果：等速率更新能精确恢复低秩目标，但随着谱尾增长其优势消失。在Transformer中，Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价的初始化——此时等变优化器仍保持浮点精度——最终各头不变量W_Q^⊤W_K的相对Frobenius距离相差56%，这是任何逐头旋转都无法弥合的差距。在两个高光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失下，梯度下降在最低采样密度和更低有效秩处将留出错误降低43-44%。因此，基座选择并非调参细节，而是关于优化器选择哪个插值函数的一项决策。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [DefoEye: Python-Based Software for Facilitating Time-Series InSAR Analysis of Sentinel-1 Remote-Sensing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04915)
*Alireza Taheri Dehkordi,Hossein Hashemi,Amir Naghibi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DefoEye (v1)，一个基于Python的开源软件包，它封装了GMTSAR并提供统一、用户友好的时序InSAR（TS-InSAR）工作流，支持并行作业执行、干涉图网络修剪和多种锚定选项。在意大利博洛尼亚、瑞典哥特兰、美国休斯顿和伊朗卡拉吉四个不同地质环境下与GNSS及现有工具对比验证，RMSE为4.3-11.9 mm，相关系数0.63-0.98，证明其可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TS-InSAR软件工具存在地理适用性受限、商业许可限制、端到端处理支持不完整等问题。虽然GMTSAR避免了部分限制，但仍需大量手动干预和C-shell命令，缺少用户友好的图形界面，且遗漏了干涉图网络修剪和未解缠干涉图锚定等关键步骤。因此需要开发一个开源、用户友好且功能完整的TS-InSAR软件包。

Method: DefoEye是一个基于Python的开源软件包，封装GMTSAR并提供统一的TS-InSAR工作流，支持Sentinel-1数据。其核心功能包括：并行作业执行以提高处理效率、干涉图网络修剪以优化网络质量、以及多种解缠干涉图锚定选项。软件在四个不同地质条件、形变机制和大气气候环境的区域进行了性能评估。

Result: 在博洛尼亚（意大利）、哥特兰（瑞典）和休斯顿（美国）三个区域，DefoEye结果与10个GNSS站观测数据高度一致，RMSE为4.3-11.9 mm，Pearson相关系数为0.63-0.95。在卡拉吉（伊朗）区域，因无GNSS数据，DefoEye与广泛使用的处理工具对比，RMSE为4.8 mm/yr，Pearson相关系数为0.98。

Conclusion: DefoEye为地质、水文和环境应用提供了可靠的TS-InSAR产品，证明了其作为开源工具在多种地质与气候条件下的有效性和实用性，弥补了现有TS-InSAR软件在易用性和功能完整性方面的不足。

Original Abstract: Many existing time-series Interferometric Synthetic Aperture Radar (TS-InSAR) software tools have limitations, including restricted geographic applicability, commercial licensing, and incomplete end-to-end processing support. Although GMTSAR avoids some of these constraints, it still requires substantial manual intervention and C-shell commands, lacks a user-friendly graphical interface, and omits important steps such as interferogram network pruning and anchoring of unwrapped interferograms. This paper introduces DefoEye (v1), an open-source Python-based software package that wraps GMTSAR and provides a unified, user-friendly TS-InSAR workflow for Sentinel-1 data. DefoEye supports parallel job execution, interferogram network pruning, and multiple anchoring options. Its performance was evaluated from 2020 to 2024 in four regions with different geological settings, deformation mechanisms, and atmospheric and climatic conditions. In Bologna, Italy; Gotland, Sweden; and Houston, USA, DefoEye results were compared with observations from 10 GNSS stations and showed strong agreement, with RMSE values of 4.3-11.9 mm and Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.63-0.95. In Karaj, Iran, where GNSS observations were unavailable, DefoEye was compared with other widely used processing tools and achieved similarly close agreement, with an RMSE of 4.8 mm/yr and a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.98. These results demonstrate that DefoEye provides reliable TS-InSAR products for geological, hydrological, and environmental applications.

中文摘要翻译: 许多现有的时序干涉合成孔径雷达（TS-InSAR）软件工具存在局限性，包括地理适用性受限、商业许可限制以及端到端处理支持不完整。尽管GMTSAR避免了部分这些限制，它仍然需要大量手动干预和C-shell命令，缺乏用户友好的图形界面，并且遗漏了干涉图网络修剪和未解缠干涉图锚定等重要步骤。本文介绍了DefoEye（v1），一个基于Python的开源软件包，它封装了GMTSAR并为Sentinel-1数据提供了统一、用户友好的TS-InSAR工作流。DefoEye支持并行作业执行、干涉图网络修剪和多种锚定选项。其性能在2020年至2024年间于四个具有不同地质构造、形变机制以及大气和气候条件的区域进行了评估。在意大利博洛尼亚、瑞典哥特兰和美国休斯顿，DefoEye的结果与10个GNSS站的观测数据进行了比较，显示出高度一致性，RMSE值为4.3-11.9 mm，Pearson相关系数为0.63-0.95。在伊朗卡拉吉，由于缺乏GNSS观测数据，DefoEye与其他广泛使用的处理工具进行了比较，同样达到了高度一致的结果，RMSE为4.8 mm/yr，Pearson相关系数为0.98。这些结果表明，DefoEye为地质、水文和环境应用提供了可靠的TS-InSAR产品。

</details>


### [223] [AI-based single-shot structured-light depth reconstruction for real-time laparoscopic surgical guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05109)
*Wayne Wonseok Rodgers,Xiangyi Le,Seonghoon Jang,Shuwen Wei,Justin Opfermann,Michael Kam,Axel Krieger,Jin U. Kang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需同步、单次拍摄的腹腔镜深度感知平台，采用被动LED照明的二进制掩膜投影模块，结合VQ-VAE先验与自定义U-Net深度头，在潜在空间中进行深度重建，无需显式分割分支，实现了视频速率的深度估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器人腹腔镜手术中需要精确的术中深度感知，但传统条纹投影轮廓术虽精度可达毫米级，却常需多次采集、DMD投影和投影仪-相机同步，难以集成到紧凑的腹腔镜系统中。因此需要一种无需同步、单次拍摄的深度感知方案。

Method: 将紧凑型投影模块耦合到双通道腹腔镜的一个通道，另一个通道拍摄条纹照射的目标。使用Zivid 3D相机获取722幅体模图像的参考深度，并将其重投影到SSLE图像帧进行监督训练。VQ-VAE将输入编码为离散潜在表示，潜在空间U-Net直接预测深度，无需单独的掩膜预测分支。

Result: 模型在固定训练/验证/测试划分下实现MAE 3.70 mm、AbsRel 0.0326、delta=1.1精度0.962和delta=1.1^2精度0.970。MAE低于双U-Net MaskNet+DepthNet基线，且在MAE、AbsRel和阈值精度上优于现成的单目深度模型。流水线在NVIDIA A100 GPU上以26.0 Hz处理301帧连续图像。

Conclusion: LED照明的二进制图案平台结合潜在空间深度重建，实现了无需同步、视频速率的腔镜深度估计。结果展示了无需显式分割阶段的Zivid参考体模重建效果，同时强调了数据集规模和SSLE-Zivid标定精度的重要性。

Original Abstract: Significance. Accurate intraoperative depth perception is important for autonomous and semi-autonomous robotic laparoscopic surgery. Conventional fringe projection profilometry can achieve millimeter-scale accuracy but often requires multi-shot acquisition, digital-micromirror-device projection, and projector-camera synchronization, complicating integration into compact laparoscopic systems.
  Aim. To develop a synchronization-free, single-shot depth-sensing platform using a passive LED-illuminated binary mask and a VQ-VAE prior with a custom U-Net depth head.
  Approach. A compact projection module was coupled to one channel of a dual-channel laparoscope, while the second channel imaged the fringe-illuminated target. A Zivid 3D camera acquired reference depth for 722 paired phantom images. Zivid depth maps were reprojected into the SSLE image frame for supervised training and evaluation. The VQ-VAE encoded each input into a discrete latent representation, and a latent-space U-Net predicted depth without a separate mask-prediction branch.
  Results. Using a fixed train/validation/test split, the proposed model achieved an MAE of 3.70 mm, AbsRel of 0.0326, delta=1.1 accuracy of 0.962, and delta=1.1^2 accuracy of 0.970. It achieved lower MAE than the dual U-Net MaskNet + DepthNet baseline and outperformed off-the-shelf monocular depth models in MAE, AbsRel, and threshold accuracy. The pipeline operated at 26.0 Hz over 301 consecutive frames on an NVIDIA A100 GPU.
  Conclusions. The LED-illuminated binary-pattern platform with latent-space depth reconstruction enables synchronization-free, video-rate endoscopic depth estimation. Results demonstrate Zivid-referenced phantom reconstruction without an explicit segmentation stage, while emphasizing the importance of dataset size and SSLE-Zivid calibration accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 意义。准确的术中深度感知对于自主和半自主机器人腹腔镜手术至关重要。传统条纹投影轮廓术可达到毫米级精度，但通常需要多次采集、数字微镜器件投影以及投影仪-相机同步，这使集成到紧凑型腹腔镜系统中变得复杂。目的。开发一种无需同步、单次拍摄的深度感知平台，采用无源LED照明二进制掩膜和带自定义U-Net深度头的VQ-VAE先验。方法。将紧凑型投影模块耦合到双通道腹腔镜的一个通道，第二个通道对条纹照射的目标成像。Zivid 3D相机获取722幅体模图像的参考深度。将Zivid深度图重投影到SSLE图像帧中，用于监督训练和评估。VQ-VAE将每个输入编码为离散潜在表示，潜在空间U-Net直接预测深度，无需单独的掩膜预测分支。结果。在固定训练/验证/测试划分下，所提出的模型实现了MAE 3.70 mm、AbsRel 0.0326、delta=1.1精度0.962和delta=1.1²精度0.970。其MAE低于双U-Net MaskNet+DepthNet基线，且在MAE、AbsRel和阈值精度上优于现成的单目深度模型。该流水线在NVIDIA A100 GPU上以26.0 Hz处理301帧连续图像。结论。LED照明的二进制图案平台结合潜在空间深度重建，可实现无需同步、视频速率的腔镜深度估计。结果表明无需显式分割阶段即可完成Zivid参考的体模重建，同时强调数据集规模和SSLE-Zivid标定精度的重要性。

</details>
